# The database objects the migrations own (Issue #152), one per line: packages/db/src/fingerprint.ts says what is covered and what is normalised.
# `pnpm db:fingerprint --write` regenerates this file from a freshly migrated database; `pnpm db:fingerprint --check` compares a database with it.
schema drizzle owner=<owner> acl=[<owner>=CREATE,USAGE/<owner>]
schema pgboss owner=<owner> acl=[<owner>=CREATE,USAGE/<owner> wms_api=USAGE/<owner> wms_worker=USAGE/<owner>]
schema wms owner=<owner> acl=[<owner>=CREATE,USAGE/<owner> wms_api=USAGE/<owner> wms_public=USAGE/<owner> wms_sync=USAGE/<owner> wms_worker=USAGE/<owner>]
schema extensions service-role-grants=[wms_api=USAGE wms_worker=USAGE]
extension btree_gist schema=extensions
extension postgis schema=extensions
role wms_api superuser=false inherit=true createrole=false createdb=false replication=false bypassrls=false connlimit=-1
role wms_sync superuser=false inherit=true createrole=false createdb=false replication=true bypassrls=true connlimit=-1
role wms_worker superuser=false inherit=true createrole=false createdb=false replication=false bypassrls=true connlimit=-1
role-setting wms_api database=* search_path=wms, extensions
role-setting wms_worker database=* search_path=wms, extensions
membership wms_api member=<owner> grantor=<owner> admin=false inherit=true set=true
membership wms_api member=<owner> grantor=supabase_admin admin=true inherit=false set=false
membership wms_public member=wms_api grantor=<owner> admin=false inherit=false set=true
membership wms_sync member=<owner> grantor=<owner> admin=false inherit=true set=true
membership wms_sync member=<owner> grantor=supabase_admin admin=true inherit=false set=false

constraint pgboss.job_common dlq_fkey FOREIGN KEY (dead_letter) REFERENCES pgboss.queue(name) ON DELETE RESTRICT DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED deferrable initially-deferred
constraint pgboss.job_common job_common_pkey PRIMARY KEY (name, id)
constraint pgboss.job_common job_key_strict_fifo_singleton_key_check CHECK ((NOT ((policy = 'key_strict_fifo'::text) AND (singleton_key IS NULL))))
constraint pgboss.job_common q_fkey FOREIGN KEY (name) REFERENCES pgboss.queue(name) ON DELETE RESTRICT DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED deferrable initially-deferred
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i1 CREATE UNIQUE INDEX job_common_i1 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, COALESCE(singleton_key, ''::text)) WHERE ((state = 'created'::pgboss.job_state) AND (policy = 'short'::text))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i10 CREATE INDEX job_common_i10 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, singleton_key, state DESC, created_on, id) INCLUDE (start_after) WHERE ((state < 'active'::pgboss.job_state) AND (NOT blocked) AND (policy = 'key_strict_fifo'::text))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i11 CREATE INDEX job_common_i11 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, priority DESC, created_on, start_after) WHERE ((state < 'active'::pgboss.job_state) AND (NOT blocked))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i2 CREATE UNIQUE INDEX job_common_i2 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, COALESCE(singleton_key, ''::text)) WHERE ((state = 'active'::pgboss.job_state) AND (policy = 'singleton'::text))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i3 CREATE UNIQUE INDEX job_common_i3 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, state, COALESCE(singleton_key, ''::text)) WHERE ((state <= 'active'::pgboss.job_state) AND (policy = 'stately'::text))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i4 CREATE UNIQUE INDEX job_common_i4 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, singleton_on, COALESCE(singleton_key, ''::text)) WHERE ((state <> 'cancelled'::pgboss.job_state) AND (singleton_on IS NOT NULL))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i6 CREATE UNIQUE INDEX job_common_i6 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, COALESCE(singleton_key, ''::text)) WHERE ((state <= 'active'::pgboss.job_state) AND (policy = 'exclusive'::text))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i7 CREATE INDEX job_common_i7 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, group_id) WHERE ((state = 'active'::pgboss.job_state) AND (group_id IS NOT NULL))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i8 CREATE UNIQUE INDEX job_common_i8 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, singleton_key) WHERE ((state = ANY (ARRAY['active'::pgboss.job_state, 'retry'::pgboss.job_state, 'failed'::pgboss.job_state])) AND (policy = 'key_strict_fifo'::text))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_i9 CREATE INDEX job_common_i9 ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, id) WHERE (blocking AND (state = 'completed'::pgboss.job_state))
index pgboss.job_common job_common_pkey CREATE UNIQUE INDEX job_common_pkey ON pgboss.job_common USING btree (name, id)

relation wms.access_credential kind=table owner=<owner> persistence=p rls=true force-rls=true replica-identity=d acl=[<owner>=DELETE,INSERT,MAINTAIN,REFERENCES,SELECT,TRIGGER,TRUNCATE,UPDATE/<owner> wms_api=DELETE,INSERT,SELECT,UPDATE/<owner> wms_worker=SELECT/<owner>]
column wms.access_credential 1 id uuid not-null default=wms.uuidv7()
column wms.access_credential 2 company_id uuid not-null
column wms.access_credential 3 project_id uuid not-null
column wms.access_credential 4 created_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.access_credential 5 updated_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.access_credential 6 valid_from date not-null
column wms.access_credential 7 valid_to date
column wms.access_credential 8 kind text not-null
column wms.access_credential 9 value text not-null
column wms.access_credential 10 label text
column wms.access_credential 11 customer_id uuid
column wms.access_credential 12 agreement_id uuid
column wms.access_credential 13 property_id uuid
column wms.access_credential 14 blocked_at timestamp with time zone
column wms.access_credential 15 blocked_reason text
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_agreement_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, agreement_id) REFERENCES wms.agreement(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_blocked_shape CHECK (((blocked_at IS NULL) = (blocked_reason IS NULL)))
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_customer_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, customer_id) REFERENCES wms.customer(company_id, id)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_holder_shape CHECK (
CASE
    WHEN (property_id IS NOT NULL) THEN ((customer_id IS NULL) AND (agreement_id IS NULL))
    ELSE ((customer_id IS NOT NULL) AND (agreement_id IS NOT NULL))
END)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['plate'::text, 'card'::text])))
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (company_id WITH =, project_id WITH =, kind WITH =, value WITH =, daterange(valid_from, valid_to, '[)'::text) WITH &&)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_property_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, property_id) REFERENCES wms.property(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_validity CHECK (((valid_to IS NULL) OR (valid_to > valid_from)))
constraint wms.access_credential access_credential_value_shape CHECK ((value ~ '^[A-Z0-9]+$'::text))
index wms.access_credential access_credential_agreement_id_idx CREATE INDEX access_credential_agreement_id_idx ON wms.access_credential USING btree (company_id, agreement_id)
index wms.access_credential access_credential_customer_id_idx CREATE INDEX access_credential_customer_id_idx ON wms.access_credential USING btree (company_id, customer_id)
index wms.access_credential access_credential_no_overlap CREATE INDEX access_credential_no_overlap ON wms.access_credential USING gist (company_id, project_id, kind, value, daterange(valid_from, valid_to, '[)'::text))
index wms.access_credential access_credential_pkey CREATE UNIQUE INDEX access_credential_pkey ON wms.access_credential USING btree (id)
index wms.access_credential access_credential_project_key CREATE UNIQUE INDEX access_credential_project_key ON wms.access_credential USING btree (company_id, project_id, id)
index wms.access_credential access_credential_property_id_idx CREATE INDEX access_credential_property_id_idx ON wms.access_credential USING btree (company_id, property_id)
trigger wms.access_credential access_credential_audit_trail enabled=O CREATE TRIGGER access_credential_audit_trail AFTER INSERT OR DELETE OR UPDATE ON wms.access_credential FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION wms.audit_row('', '')
trigger wms.access_credential access_credential_touch_updated_at enabled=O CREATE TRIGGER access_credential_touch_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON wms.access_credential FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION wms.touch_updated_at()

column wms.agreement 1 id uuid not-null default=wms.uuidv7()
column wms.agreement 2 company_id uuid not-null
column wms.agreement 3 project_id uuid not-null
column wms.agreement 4 created_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.agreement 5 updated_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.agreement 6 valid_from date not-null
column wms.agreement 7 valid_to date
column wms.agreement 8 number text not-null
column wms.agreement 9 customer_id uuid not-null
column wms.agreement 10 payer_customer_id uuid not-null
column wms.agreement 11 status text not-null
column wms.agreement 12 billing_cadence text not-null
column wms.agreement 13 currency text not-null
column wms.agreement 14 notes text
column wms.agreement 15 price_list_id uuid
column wms.agreement 16 service_window_start time without time zone
column wms.agreement 17 service_window_end time without time zone
constraint wms.agreement agreement_billing_cadence_one_of CHECK ((billing_cadence = ANY (ARRAY['monthly'::text, 'quarterly'::text, 'annual'::text, 'manual'::text])))
constraint wms.agreement agreement_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_customer_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, customer_id) REFERENCES wms.customer(company_id, id)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (company_id WITH =, number WITH =, daterange(valid_from, valid_to, '[)'::text) WITH &&)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_payer_customer_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, payer_customer_id) REFERENCES wms.customer(company_id, id)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_price_list_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, price_list_id) REFERENCES wms.price_list(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.agreement agreement_service_window CHECK ((((service_window_start IS NULL) = (service_window_end IS NULL)) AND ((service_window_start IS NULL) OR (service_window_end > service_window_start))))
constraint wms.agreement agreement_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['draft'::text, 'active'::text, 'cancelled'::text])))
constraint wms.agreement agreement_validity CHECK (((valid_to IS NULL) OR (valid_to > valid_from)))

column wms.billable_event 31 charge_period_to date
column wms.billable_event 32 charged_days integer
column wms.billable_event 33 period_days integer
column wms.billable_event 34 container_id uuid
column wms.billable_event 35 placement_id uuid
column wms.billable_event 36 evidence_proof_id uuid
column wms.billable_event 37 held_at timestamp with time zone
column wms.billable_event 38 held_by uuid
column wms.billable_event 39 hold_reason text
column wms.billable_event 40 visit_id uuid
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_agreement_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, agreement_id) REFERENCES wms.agreement(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_amounts_shape CHECK (((net_minor IS NULL) OR (net_minor = ((
CASE kind
    WHEN 'reversal'::text THEN '-1'::integer
    ELSE 1
END * (unit_price_minor)::bigint) * quantity))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_block_reason_one_of CHECK ((block_reason = ANY (ARRAY['no-subscription'::text, 'agreement-draft'::text, 'no-price-list'::text, 'no-price-row'::text, 'no-product'::text, 'no-vat-rate'::text, 'amount-too-large'::text])))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_cancel_reason_one_of CHECK ((cancel_reason = ANY (ARRAY['pickup-corrected'::text, 'visit-voided'::text, 'duplicate'::text, 'not-delivered'::text, 'other'::text])))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_cancel_shape CHECK ((((cancelled_at IS NULL) = (cancel_reason IS NULL)) AND ((cancelled_by IS NULL) OR (cancelled_at IS NOT NULL))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_cancelled_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, cancelled_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_charge_shape CHECK ((((kind = ANY (ARRAY['subscription'::text, 'hire'::text])) = (charge_period_from IS NOT NULL)) AND ((kind = 'hire'::text) = (placement_id IS NOT NULL)) AND ((placement_id IS NULL) = (container_id IS NULL)) AND ((charge_period_from IS NULL) = (charge_period_to IS NULL)) AND ((charge_period_from IS NULL) = (charged_days IS NULL)) AND ((charge_period_from IS NULL) = (period_days IS NULL)) AND ((charge_period_to IS NULL) OR (charge_period_to > charge_period_from)) AND ((charged_days IS NULL) OR ((charged_days > 0) AND (charged_days <= period_days)))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_container_id_placement_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, container_id, placement_id) REFERENCES wms.container_service_placement(company_id, project_id, container_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_created_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, created_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_evidence_shape CHECK (((evidence_proof_id IS NULL) OR (kind = 'pickup-exception'::text)))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_held_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, held_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_hold_shape CHECK ((((held_at IS NULL) = (hold_reason IS NULL)) AND ((held_by IS NULL) OR (held_at IS NOT NULL)) AND ((held_at IS NULL) OR (block_reason IS NULL))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['pickup'::text, 'ticket'::text, 'manual'::text, 'reversal'::text, 'subscription'::text, 'job'::text, 'hire'::text, 'pickup-exception'::text, 'visit'::text])))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_kind_shape CHECK (
CASE kind
    WHEN 'pickup'::text THEN ((pickup_id IS NOT NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'ticket'::text THEN ((ticket_id IS NOT NULL) AND (pickup_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'manual'::text THEN ((pickup_id IS NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL) AND (created_by IS NOT NULL))
    WHEN 'reversal'::text THEN ((reverses_event_id IS NOT NULL) AND (pickup_id IS NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (net_minor IS NOT NULL) AND (net_minor <= 0))
    WHEN 'subscription'::text THEN ((subscription_id IS NOT NULL) AND (pickup_id IS NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'job'::text THEN ((pickup_id IS NOT NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'hire'::text THEN ((subscription_id IS NOT NULL) AND (placement_id IS NOT NULL) AND (pickup_id IS NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'pickup-exception'::text THEN ((pickup_id IS NOT NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'visit'::text THEN ((visit_id IS NOT NULL) AND (pickup_id IS NULL) AND (ticket_id IS NULL) AND (reverses_event_id IS NULL))
    ELSE false
END)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_origin_shape CHECK (
CASE
    WHEN (kind = ANY (ARRAY['subscription'::text, 'hire'::text])) THEN ((created_by IS NULL) AND (source_event_id IS NULL))
    ELSE ((created_by IS NULL) = (source_event_id IS NOT NULL))
END)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_override_shape CHECK ((((kind = 'manual'::text) AND (price_list_row_id IS NULL) AND (block_reason IS NULL)) = (override_reason IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_pickup_shape CHECK (((pickup_id IS NULL) OR (route_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_price_list_row_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, price_list_row_id) REFERENCES wms.price_list_row(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_priced_references CHECK (((block_reason IS NOT NULL) OR ((agreement_id IS NOT NULL) AND (product_id IS NOT NULL))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_priced_shape CHECK ((((block_reason IS NULL) = (net_minor IS NOT NULL)) AND ((net_minor IS NULL) = (unit_price_minor IS NULL)) AND ((net_minor IS NULL) = (vat_percent IS NULL)) AND ((net_minor IS NULL) = (vat_minor IS NULL)) AND ((net_minor IS NULL) = (currency IS NULL))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_product_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, product_id) REFERENCES wms.product(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_quantity_positive CHECK ((quantity > 0))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_reverses_event_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, reverses_event_id) REFERENCES wms.billable_event(company_id, project_id, id)

constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_route_id_pickup_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_id, pickup_id) REFERENCES wms.pickup(company_id, project_id, route_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_row_shape CHECK (((price_list_row_id IS NOT NULL) OR (block_reason IS NOT NULL) OR (kind = ANY (ARRAY['manual'::text, 'reversal'::text]))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_subscription_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, subscription_id) REFERENCES wms.subscription(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_ticket_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, ticket_id) REFERENCES wms.ticket(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_vat_shape CHECK (((vat_minor IS NULL) OR ((vat_minor)::numeric = round((((net_minor)::numeric * (vat_percent)::numeric) / 100.0)))))
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_visit_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, visit_id) REFERENCES wms.site_visit(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.billable_event billable_event_visit_shape CHECK (((visit_id IS NULL) OR (kind = 'visit'::text)))

constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_holiday_policy_one_of CHECK ((holiday_policy = ANY (ARRAY['shift-next'::text, 'shift-prev'::text, 'skip'::text, 'collect'::text])))
constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (company_id WITH =, planning_area_id WITH =, daterange(valid_from, valid_to, '[)'::text) WITH &&)

constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_service_days_non_empty CHECK ((cardinality(service_days) > 0))
constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_service_days_subset_of CHECK ((service_days <@ ARRAY['monday'::text, 'tuesday'::text, 'wednesday'::text, 'thursday'::text, 'friday'::text, 'saturday'::text, 'sunday'::text]))
constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_slots_per_day_positive CHECK ((slots_per_day > 0))
constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_validity CHECK (((valid_to IS NULL) OR (valid_to > valid_from)))
constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_week_rotation_one_of CHECK ((week_rotation = ANY (ARRAY['odd'::text, 'even'::text])))
constraint wms.bulky_schedule bulky_schedule_week_rotation_shape CHECK (((frequency = 'every-2-weeks'::text) = (week_rotation IS NOT NULL)))

column wms.pickup 7 container_id uuid
column wms.pickup 8 position integer not-null

column wms.pickup 10 note text
column wms.pickup 11 property_id uuid
column wms.pickup 12 shared_collection_point_id uuid
column wms.pickup 13 waste_fraction_id uuid not-null
column wms.pickup 14 arrived_at timestamp with time zone
column wms.pickup 15 outcome_at timestamp with time zone

column wms.pickup 19 subscription_id uuid
column wms.pickup 20 warehouse_id uuid
column wms.pickup 21 replacement_container_id uuid
column wms.pickup 22 reserved_container_id uuid generated=(
CASE
    WHEN (status = 'planned'::text) THEN
    CASE service
        WHEN 'deliver'::text THEN container_id
        WHEN 'swap'::text THEN replacement_container_id
        ELSE NULL::uuid
    END
    ELSE NULL::uuid
END)
column wms.pickup 23 taken_container_id uuid generated=(
CASE
    WHEN ((status = 'planned'::text) AND (service = ANY (ARRAY['remove'::text, 'swap'::text]))) THEN container_id
    ELSE NULL::uuid
END)

column wms.plan 5 updated_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.plan 6 route_id uuid not-null
column wms.plan 7 solver text not-null
column wms.plan 8 status text not-null default='calculating'::text
column wms.plan 9 fingerprint text not-null
column wms.plan 10 trip text not-null
column wms.plan 11 distance_metres integer
column wms.plan 12 duration_seconds integer
column wms.plan 13 deferred_until timestamp with time zone
column wms.plan 14 failure_reason text
column wms.plan 15 provider text not-null
column wms.plan 16 engine_version text
column wms.plan 17 graph_date date
column wms.plan 18 waiting_seconds integer
constraint wms.plan plan_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.plan plan_deferred_shape CHECK (((deferred_until IS NULL) OR (status = 'calculating'::text)))
constraint wms.plan plan_failure_shape CHECK (((status = 'failed'::text) = (failure_reason IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.plan plan_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.plan plan_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.plan plan_route_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_id) REFERENCES wms.route(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.plan plan_route_id_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, route_id, id)
constraint wms.plan plan_solver_one_of CHECK ((solver = ANY (ARRAY['optimiser'::text, 'manual'::text, 'baseline'::text])))
constraint wms.plan plan_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['calculating'::text, 'ready'::text, 'failed'::text])))
constraint wms.plan plan_totals_shape CHECK (
CASE status
    WHEN 'ready'::text THEN ((distance_metres IS NOT NULL) AND (distance_metres >= 0) AND (duration_seconds IS NOT NULL) AND (duration_seconds >= 0))
    ELSE ((distance_metres IS NULL) AND (duration_seconds IS NULL))
END)
constraint wms.plan plan_trip_one_of CHECK ((trip = ANY (ARRAY['full'::text, 'stops-only'::text])))
constraint wms.plan plan_waiting_shape CHECK (((waiting_seconds IS NULL) OR ((waiting_seconds >= 0) AND (status = 'ready'::text) AND (solver = 'optimiser'::text))))

relation wms.proof_of_service kind=table owner=<owner> persistence=p rls=true force-rls=true replica-identity=d acl=[<owner>=DELETE,INSERT,MAINTAIN,REFERENCES,SELECT,TRIGGER,TRUNCATE,UPDATE/<owner> wms_api=INSERT,SELECT/<owner> wms_sync=SELECT/<owner> wms_worker=SELECT/<owner>]
column wms.proof_of_service 1 id uuid not-null default=wms.uuidv7()
column wms.proof_of_service 2 company_id uuid not-null
column wms.proof_of_service 3 project_id uuid not-null
column wms.proof_of_service 4 recorded_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.proof_of_service 5 route_id uuid not-null
column wms.proof_of_service 6 pickup_id uuid
column wms.proof_of_service 7 session_id uuid
column wms.proof_of_service 8 kind text not-null
column wms.proof_of_service 9 source text not-null
column wms.proof_of_service 10 occurred_at timestamp with time zone not-null
column wms.proof_of_service 11 recorded_by uuid not-null
column wms.proof_of_service 12 device_id text
column wms.proof_of_service 13 location extensions.geometry(Point,4326)
column wms.proof_of_service 14 location_accuracy_m integer
column wms.proof_of_service 15 reason text
column wms.proof_of_service 16 note text
column wms.proof_of_service 17 weight_kg integer
column wms.proof_of_service 18 object_key text
column wms.proof_of_service 19 outcome text
column wms.proof_of_service 20 contaminant_code_id uuid
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_contaminant_code_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, contaminant_code_id) REFERENCES wms.contaminant_code(company_id, id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_contaminant_code_shape CHECK (((contaminant_code_id IS NULL) OR (kind = ANY (ARRAY['completion'::text, 'skip'::text, 'failure'::text, 'correction'::text]))))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['arrival'::text, 'completion'::text, 'skip'::text, 'failure'::text, 'problem'::text, 'route-started'::text, 'route-ended'::text, 'photo'::text, 'weight'::text, 'signature'::text, 'note'::text, 'correction'::text])))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_kind_shape CHECK (
CASE kind
    WHEN 'arrival'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'completion'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'skip'::text THEN ((reason IS NOT NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'failure'::text THEN ((reason IS NOT NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'problem'::text THEN ((reason IS NOT NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL) AND (note IS NOT NULL))
    WHEN 'route-started'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'route-ended'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'photo'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NOT NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'weight'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NOT NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'signature'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NOT NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL))
    WHEN 'note'::text THEN ((reason IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NULL) AND (note IS NOT NULL))
    WHEN 'correction'::text THEN ((object_key IS NULL) AND (weight_kg IS NULL) AND (outcome IS NOT NULL) AND (note IS NOT NULL) AND (source = 'dispatch'::text))
    ELSE false
END)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_location_accuracy_m_positive CHECK ((location_accuracy_m > 0))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_location_valid CHECK ((extensions.st_isvalid(location) AND (NOT extensions.st_isempty(location)) AND (extensions.st_xmin((location)::extensions.box3d) >= ('-180'::integer)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_xmax((location)::extensions.box3d) <= (180)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_ymin((location)::extensions.box3d) >= ('-90'::integer)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_ymax((location)::extensions.box3d) <= (90)::double precision)))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_outcome_one_of CHECK ((outcome = ANY (ARRAY['completed'::text, 'skipped'::text, 'failed'::text])))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_pickup_shape CHECK ((((kind = ANY (ARRAY['arrival'::text, 'completion'::text, 'skip'::text, 'failure'::text, 'weight'::text, 'signature'::text, 'correction'::text])) AND (pickup_id IS NOT NULL)) OR ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['route-started'::text, 'route-ended'::text])) AND (pickup_id IS NULL)) OR (kind = ANY (ARRAY['problem'::text, 'photo'::text, 'note'::text]))))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_reason_one_of CHECK ((reason = ANY (ARRAY['inaccessible'::text, 'contamination'::text, 'not-presented'::text, 'capacity'::text, 'safety'::text, 'other'::text, 'route-ended'::text, 'route-cancelled'::text, 'removed-by-dispatcher'::text, 'regeneration'::text])))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_recorded_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, recorded_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_route_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_id) REFERENCES wms.route(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_route_id_pickup_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_id, pickup_id) REFERENCES wms.pickup(company_id, project_id, route_id, id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_route_id_session_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_id, session_id) REFERENCES wms.session(company_id, project_id, route_id, id)
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_session_shape CHECK (((source = 'driver-app'::text) = (session_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_source_one_of CHECK ((source = ANY (ARRAY['driver-app'::text, 'dispatch'::text, 'integration'::text])))
constraint wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_weight_kg_positive CHECK ((weight_kg > 0))
index wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_contaminant_code_id_idx CREATE INDEX proof_of_service_contaminant_code_id_idx ON wms.proof_of_service USING btree (company_id, contaminant_code_id)
index wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_pickup_id_idx CREATE INDEX proof_of_service_pickup_id_idx ON wms.proof_of_service USING btree (company_id, pickup_id)
index wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_pkey CREATE UNIQUE INDEX proof_of_service_pkey ON wms.proof_of_service USING btree (id)
index wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_project_id_idx CREATE INDEX proof_of_service_project_id_idx ON wms.proof_of_service USING btree (company_id, project_id)
index wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_recorded_by_idx CREATE INDEX proof_of_service_recorded_by_idx ON wms.proof_of_service USING btree (company_id, recorded_by)
index wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_route_id_idx CREATE INDEX proof_of_service_route_id_idx ON wms.proof_of_service USING btree (company_id, route_id, id)
index wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_session_id_idx CREATE INDEX proof_of_service_session_id_idx ON wms.proof_of_service USING btree (company_id, session_id)
policy wms.proof_of_service proof_of_service_tenant_fence permissive ALL to=wms_api using=(company_id = ( SELECT wms.current_company_id() AS current_company_id)) check=(company_id = ( SELECT wms.current_company_id() AS current_company_id))
relation wms.property kind=table owner=<owner> persistence=p rls=true force-rls=true replica-identity=d acl=[<owner>=DELETE,INSERT,MAINTAIN,REFERENCES,SELECT,TRIGGER,TRUNCATE,UPDATE/<owner> wms_api=DELETE,INSERT,SELECT,UPDATE/<owner> wms_sync=SELECT/<owner> wms_worker=SELECT/<owner>]
column wms.property 1 id uuid not-null default=wms.uuidv7() acl=[wms_public=SELECT/<owner>]

column wms.property 5 updated_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.property 6 name text not-null
column wms.property 7 address text not-null acl=[wms_public=SELECT/<owner>]
column wms.property 8 registry_id text
column wms.property 9 kind text not-null
column wms.property 10 location extensions.geometry(Point,4326)
column wms.property 11 notes text

column wms.property 15 service_window_end time without time zone

column wms.property 18 house_number text
column wms.property 19 floor text
column wms.property 20 door text
column wms.property 21 postcode text
column wms.property 22 post_town text
column wms.property 23 municipality_code text
constraint wms.property property_address_shape CHECK ((((address_id IS NULL) = (street_name IS NULL)) AND ((address_id IS NULL) = (house_number IS NULL)) AND ((address_id IS NULL) = (postcode IS NULL)) AND ((address_id IS NULL) = (post_town IS NULL)) AND ((address_id IS NULL) = (municipality_code IS NULL)) AND ((address_id IS NOT NULL) OR ((floor IS NULL) AND (door IS NULL)))))
constraint wms.property property_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.property property_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['residential'::text, 'commercial'::text, 'public'::text, 'mixed'::text, 'other'::text])))
constraint wms.property property_location_valid CHECK ((extensions.st_isvalid(location) AND (NOT extensions.st_isempty(location)) AND (extensions.st_xmin((location)::extensions.box3d) >= ('-180'::integer)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_xmax((location)::extensions.box3d) <= (180)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_ymin((location)::extensions.box3d) >= ('-90'::integer)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_ymax((location)::extensions.box3d) <= (90)::double precision)))
constraint wms.property property_municipality_code_shape CHECK ((municipality_code ~ '^[0-9]{4}$'::text))
constraint wms.property property_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.property property_postcode_shape CHECK ((postcode ~ '^[0-9]{4}$'::text))
constraint wms.property property_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.property property_project_id_name_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, name)
constraint wms.property property_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.property property_service_window CHECK ((((service_window_start IS NULL) = (service_window_end IS NULL)) AND ((service_window_start IS NULL) OR (service_window_end > service_window_start))))
constraint wms.property property_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['active'::text, 'inactive'::text])))
index wms.property property_pkey CREATE UNIQUE INDEX property_pkey ON wms.property USING btree (id)
index wms.property property_project_id_name_key CREATE UNIQUE INDEX property_project_id_name_key ON wms.property USING btree (company_id, project_id, name)
index wms.property property_project_key CREATE UNIQUE INDEX property_project_key ON wms.property USING btree (company_id, project_id, id)
index wms.property property_registry_id_idx CREATE UNIQUE INDEX property_registry_id_idx ON wms.property USING btree (company_id, registry_id) WHERE (registry_id IS NOT NULL)

policy wms.property property_public_door permissive SELECT to=wms_public using=((company_id = ( SELECT wms.current_company_id() AS current_company_id)) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM wms.project published
  WHERE ((published.company_id = property.company_id) AND (published.id = property.project_id) AND (published.public_slug IS NOT NULL))))) check=none

constraint wms.route route_active_plan_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, id, active_plan_id) REFERENCES wms.plan(company_id, project_id, route_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (active_plan_id)
constraint wms.route route_actual_driver_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, actual_driver_id) REFERENCES wms.driver(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_actual_shape CHECK ((((actual_driver_id IS NOT NULL) = (started_at IS NOT NULL)) AND ((actual_vehicle_id IS NOT NULL) = (started_at IS NOT NULL)) AND ((actual_trailer_id IS NULL) OR (started_at IS NOT NULL))))
constraint wms.route route_actual_trailer_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, actual_trailer_id) REFERENCES wms.vehicle(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_actual_vehicle_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, actual_vehicle_id) REFERENCES wms.vehicle(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_cancel_reason_code_one_of CHECK ((cancel_reason_code = ANY (ARRAY['vehicle-breakdown'::text, 'crew-unavailable'::text, 'weather'::text, 'road-closure'::text, 'holiday'::text, 'duplicate'::text, 'merged'::text, 'other'::text, 'regeneration'::text])))
constraint wms.route route_cancel_reason_shape CHECK (((cancel_reason_code IS NULL) OR ((status = 'cancelled'::text) AND ((cancel_reason_code = 'regeneration'::text) = cancelled_by_generation))))
constraint wms.route route_collection_group_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, collection_group_id) REFERENCES wms.collection_group(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.route route_depot_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, depot_id) REFERENCES wms.depot(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_generation_key UNIQUE (company_id, route_scheme_id, collection_group_id, service_date)
constraint wms.route route_generation_run_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, generation_run_id) REFERENCES wms.generation_run(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_hold_shape CHECK (((held_at IS NULL) OR (status = 'planned'::text)))
constraint wms.route route_number_key UNIQUE (company_id, number)
constraint wms.route route_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.route route_planned_driver_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, planned_driver_id) REFERENCES wms.driver(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_planned_service_provider_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, planned_service_provider_id) REFERENCES wms.service_provider(company_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_planned_trailer_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, planned_trailer_id) REFERENCES wms.vehicle(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_planned_vehicle_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, planned_vehicle_id) REFERENCES wms.vehicle(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_route_scheme_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_scheme_id) REFERENCES wms.route_scheme(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.route route_scheme_shape CHECK ((((route_scheme_id IS NULL) = (collection_group_id IS NULL)) AND ((route_scheme_id IS NOT NULL) OR ((generation_run_id IS NULL) AND (NOT cancelled_by_generation)))))
constraint wms.route route_stamps_shape CHECK (
CASE status
    WHEN 'planned'::text THEN ((dispatched_at IS NULL) AND (started_at IS NULL) AND (completed_at IS NULL) AND (cancelled_at IS NULL))
    WHEN 'ready'::text THEN ((dispatched_at IS NOT NULL) AND (started_at IS NULL) AND (completed_at IS NULL) AND (cancelled_at IS NULL))
    WHEN 'active'::text THEN ((dispatched_at IS NOT NULL) AND (started_at IS NOT NULL) AND (completed_at IS NULL) AND (cancelled_at IS NULL))
    WHEN 'completed'::text THEN ((dispatched_at IS NOT NULL) AND (started_at IS NOT NULL) AND (completed_at IS NOT NULL) AND (cancelled_at IS NULL))
    WHEN 'cancelled'::text THEN ((cancelled_at IS NOT NULL) AND (completed_at IS NULL) AND ((started_at IS NULL) OR (dispatched_at IS NOT NULL)))
    ELSE false
END)
constraint wms.route route_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['planned'::text, 'ready'::text, 'active'::text, 'completed'::text, 'cancelled'::text])))
constraint wms.route route_unloading_station_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, unloading_station_id) REFERENCES wms.unloading_station(company_id, id)

column wms.service_provider_activity 12 body text
column wms.service_provider_activity 13 due_on date
column wms.service_provider_activity 14 route_scheme_id uuid
column wms.service_provider_activity 15 collection_group_id uuid
column wms.service_provider_activity 16 service_days text[]
column wms.service_provider_activity 17 effective_from date
column wms.service_provider_activity 18 successor_scheme_id uuid
column wms.service_provider_activity 19 opened_by uuid not-null
column wms.service_provider_activity 20 closed_by uuid
column wms.service_provider_activity 21 closed_at timestamp with time zone
column wms.service_provider_activity 22 close_reason text
column wms.service_provider_activity 23 vehicle_id uuid
column wms.service_provider_activity 24 driver_id uuid
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_closed_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, closed_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_closed_shape CHECK ((((status = ANY (ARRAY['pending-approval'::text, 'open'::text])) = ((closed_by IS NULL) AND (closed_at IS NULL))) AND ((status <> 'rejected'::text) OR (close_reason IS NOT NULL)) AND ((status = 'approved'::text) = (successor_scheme_id IS NOT NULL))))
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_collection_group_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, collection_group_id) REFERENCES wms.collection_group(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_driver_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, driver_id) REFERENCES wms.driver(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['route-day-change'::text, 'compliance-follow-up'::text, 'coordination-note'::text, 'vehicle-change'::text, 'stop-set-change'::text, 'waste-fraction-change'::text])))
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_kind_shape CHECK (
CASE kind
    WHEN 'route-day-change'::text THEN ((route_scheme_id IS NOT NULL) AND (collection_group_id IS NOT NULL) AND (cardinality(service_days) > 0) AND (effective_from IS NOT NULL) AND (due_on IS NULL) AND (vehicle_id IS NULL) AND (driver_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'vehicle-change'::text THEN ((route_scheme_id IS NOT NULL) AND (collection_group_id IS NOT NULL) AND (vehicle_id IS NOT NULL) AND (effective_from IS NOT NULL) AND (service_days IS NULL) AND (due_on IS NULL))
    WHEN 'waste-fraction-change'::text THEN ((route_scheme_id IS NOT NULL) AND (collection_group_id IS NOT NULL) AND (effective_from IS NOT NULL) AND (service_days IS NULL) AND (due_on IS NULL) AND (vehicle_id IS NULL) AND (driver_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'stop-set-change'::text THEN ((route_scheme_id IS NOT NULL) AND (collection_group_id IS NOT NULL) AND (effective_from IS NOT NULL) AND (service_days IS NULL) AND (due_on IS NULL) AND (vehicle_id IS NULL) AND (driver_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 'compliance-follow-up'::text THEN ((due_on IS NOT NULL) AND (route_scheme_id IS NULL) AND (collection_group_id IS NULL) AND (service_days IS NULL) AND (effective_from IS NULL) AND (vehicle_id IS NULL) AND (driver_id IS NULL))
    ELSE ((due_on IS NULL) AND (route_scheme_id IS NULL) AND (collection_group_id IS NULL) AND (service_days IS NULL) AND (effective_from IS NULL) AND (vehicle_id IS NULL) AND (driver_id IS NULL))
END)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_number_key UNIQUE (company_id, number)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_opened_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, opened_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_route_scheme_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_scheme_id) REFERENCES wms.route_scheme(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_service_area_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, service_area_id) REFERENCES wms.service_area(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_service_days_subset_of CHECK ((service_days <@ ARRAY['monday'::text, 'tuesday'::text, 'wednesday'::text, 'thursday'::text, 'friday'::text, 'saturday'::text, 'sunday'::text]))
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_service_provider_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, service_provider_id) REFERENCES wms.service_provider(company_id, id)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['pending-approval'::text, 'approved'::text, 'rejected'::text, 'withdrawn'::text, 'open'::text, 'completed'::text, 'cancelled'::text])))
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_status_shape CHECK (
CASE
    WHEN (kind = ANY (ARRAY['route-day-change'::text, 'vehicle-change'::text, 'stop-set-change'::text, 'waste-fraction-change'::text])) THEN (status = ANY (ARRAY['pending-approval'::text, 'approved'::text, 'rejected'::text, 'withdrawn'::text]))
    ELSE (status = ANY (ARRAY['open'::text, 'completed'::text, 'cancelled'::text]))
END)
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_subject_given CHECK ((length(btrim(subject)) > 0))
constraint wms.service_provider_activity service_provider_activity_successor_scheme_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, successor_scheme_id) REFERENCES wms.route_scheme(company_id, project_id, id)

constraint wms.settlement settlement_stamps_shape CHECK (
CASE status
    WHEN 'open'::text THEN ((calculated_at IS NULL) AND (closed_at IS NULL) AND (closed_by IS NULL))
    WHEN 'calculated'::text THEN ((calculated_at IS NOT NULL) AND (closed_at IS NULL) AND (closed_by IS NULL))
    WHEN 'closed'::text THEN ((calculated_at IS NOT NULL) AND (closed_at IS NOT NULL) AND (closed_by IS NOT NULL))
    ELSE false
END)
constraint wms.settlement settlement_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['open'::text, 'calculated'::text, 'closed'::text])))
constraint wms.settlement settlement_validity CHECK (((valid_to IS NULL) OR (valid_to > valid_from)))

constraint wms.site_visit site_visit_charge_n_positive CHECK ((charge_n > 0))
constraint wms.site_visit site_visit_charge_product_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, charge_product_id) REFERENCES wms.product(company_id, project_id, id)

constraint wms.site_visit site_visit_holder_shape CHECK (((NOT ((property_id IS NOT NULL) AND (customer_id IS NOT NULL))) AND ((customer_id IS NULL) OR (agreement_id IS NOT NULL)) AND ((outcome <> 'admitted'::text) OR (agreement_id IS NOT NULL))))
constraint wms.site_visit site_visit_identification_one_of CHECK ((identification = ANY (ARRAY['plate'::text, 'card'::text, 'property'::text])))
constraint wms.site_visit site_visit_identifier_shape CHECK (((identification = 'property'::text) = (identifier_value IS NULL)))

constraint wms.site_visit site_visit_outcome_shape CHECK (((outcome = 'refused'::text) = (refusal_reason IS NOT NULL)))

    WHEN 'staff'::text THEN ((recorded_by IS NOT NULL) AND (external_id IS NULL))
    ELSE (recorded_by IS NULL)

relation wms.stock_movement kind=table owner=<owner> persistence=p rls=true force-rls=true replica-identity=d acl=[<owner>=DELETE,INSERT,MAINTAIN,REFERENCES,SELECT,TRIGGER,TRUNCATE,UPDATE/<owner> wms_api=INSERT,SELECT/<owner> wms_worker=SELECT/<owner>]
column wms.stock_movement 1 id uuid not-null default=wms.uuidv7()
column wms.stock_movement 2 company_id uuid not-null
column wms.stock_movement 3 project_id uuid not-null
column wms.stock_movement 4 recorded_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.stock_movement 5 container_id uuid not-null
column wms.stock_movement 6 kind text not-null
column wms.stock_movement 7 from_kind text not-null
column wms.stock_movement 8 from_warehouse_id uuid
column wms.stock_movement 9 to_kind text not-null
column wms.stock_movement 10 to_warehouse_id uuid
column wms.stock_movement 11 placement_id uuid
column wms.stock_movement 12 occurred_at timestamp with time zone not-null
column wms.stock_movement 13 recorded_by uuid not-null
column wms.stock_movement 14 reason text
column wms.stock_movement 15 reference text
column wms.stock_movement 16 corrects_movement_id uuid
column wms.stock_movement 17 decommission_reason_id uuid
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_container_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, container_id) REFERENCES wms.container(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_container_id_placement_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, container_id, placement_id) REFERENCES wms.container_service_placement(company_id, project_id, container_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_corrects_movement_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, corrects_movement_id) REFERENCES wms.stock_movement(company_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_decommission_reason_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, decommission_reason_id) REFERENCES wms.decommission_reason(company_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_decommission_reason_shape CHECK (((kind = 'decommission'::text) = (decommission_reason_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_from_kind_one_of CHECK ((from_kind = ANY (ARRAY['supplier'::text, 'warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text, 'service'::text, 'scrap'::text])))
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_from_shape CHECK (((from_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text])) = (from_warehouse_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_from_warehouse_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, from_warehouse_id) REFERENCES wms.warehouse(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['receipt'::text, 'issue'::text, 'return'::text, 'transfer'::text, 'adjustment'::text, 'decommission'::text])))
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_kind_shape CHECK (
CASE kind
    WHEN 'receipt'::text THEN ((from_kind = 'supplier'::text) AND (to_kind = 'warehouse'::text))
    WHEN 'issue'::text THEN ((from_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text])) AND (to_kind = 'service'::text))
    WHEN 'return'::text THEN ((from_kind = 'service'::text) AND (to_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text])))
    WHEN 'transfer'::text THEN ((from_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text])) AND (to_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text])))
    WHEN 'decommission'::text THEN ((from_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text, 'service'::text])) AND (to_kind = 'scrap'::text))
    WHEN 'adjustment'::text THEN ((from_kind <> 'service'::text) AND (to_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text, 'scrap'::text])))
    ELSE false
END)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_placement_shape CHECK ((((from_kind = 'service'::text) OR (to_kind = 'service'::text)) = (placement_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_recorded_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, recorded_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_tenant_key UNIQUE (company_id, id)
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_to_kind_one_of CHECK ((to_kind = ANY (ARRAY['supplier'::text, 'warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text, 'service'::text, 'scrap'::text])))
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_to_shape CHECK (((to_kind = ANY (ARRAY['warehouse'::text, 'maintenance'::text])) = (to_warehouse_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.stock_movement stock_movement_to_warehouse_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, to_warehouse_id) REFERENCES wms.warehouse(company_id, project_id, id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_container_id_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_container_id_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, container_id, id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_corrects_movement_id_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_corrects_movement_id_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, corrects_movement_id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_decommission_reason_id_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_decommission_reason_id_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, decommission_reason_id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_from_warehouse_id_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_from_warehouse_id_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, from_warehouse_id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_pkey CREATE UNIQUE INDEX stock_movement_pkey ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_placement_id_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_placement_id_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, placement_id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_project_id_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_project_id_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, project_id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_recorded_by_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_recorded_by_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, recorded_by)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_tenant_key CREATE UNIQUE INDEX stock_movement_tenant_key ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, id)
index wms.stock_movement stock_movement_to_warehouse_id_idx CREATE INDEX stock_movement_to_warehouse_id_idx ON wms.stock_movement USING btree (company_id, to_warehouse_id)
policy wms.stock_movement stock_movement_tenant_fence permissive ALL to=wms_api using=(company_id = ( SELECT wms.current_company_id() AS current_company_id)) check=(company_id = ( SELECT wms.current_company_id() AS current_company_id))

column wms.subscription 10 property_id uuid acl=[wms_public=SELECT/<owner>]

constraint wms.subscription subscription_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.subscription subscription_location_exactly_one CHECK (((((property_id IS NOT NULL))::integer + ((shared_collection_point_id IS NOT NULL))::integer) = 1))
constraint wms.subscription subscription_no_overlap EXCLUDE USING gist (company_id WITH =, agreement_id WITH =, product_id WITH =, location_id WITH =, daterange(valid_from, valid_to, '[)'::text) WITH &&)

constraint wms.subscription subscription_property_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, property_id) REFERENCES wms.property(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.subscription subscription_quantity_positive CHECK ((quantity > 0))

constraint wms.subscription subscription_validity CHECK (((valid_to IS NULL) OR (valid_to > valid_from)))

policy wms.subscription subscription_public_door permissive SELECT to=wms_public using=((company_id = ( SELECT wms.current_company_id() AS current_company_id)) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM wms.project published
  WHERE ((published.company_id = subscription.company_id) AND (published.id = subscription.project_id) AND (published.public_slug IS NOT NULL))))) check=none

constraint wms.ticket ticket_agreement_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, agreement_id) REFERENCES wms.agreement(company_id, project_id, id)

constraint wms.ticket ticket_booked_route_id_booked_pickup_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, booked_route_id, booked_pickup_id) REFERENCES wms.pickup(company_id, project_id, route_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_booked_shape CHECK (((booked_route_id IS NULL) = (booked_pickup_id IS NULL)))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_charge_product_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, charge_product_id) REFERENCES wms.product(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_charge_shape CHECK (((charge_product_id IS NULL) OR ((resolution IS NOT NULL) AND (resolution = ANY (ARRAY['recollected'::text, 'serviced'::text])))))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_closed_shape CHECK (((status = ANY (ARRAY['completed'::text, 'rejected'::text])) = (closed_at IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_container_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, container_id) REFERENCES wms.container(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_created_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, created_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_customer_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, customer_id) REFERENCES wms.customer(company_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_driver_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, driver_id) REFERENCES wms.driver(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['missed-collection'::text, 'overflow'::text, 'access-issue'::text, 'container-request'::text, 'container-defect'::text, 'proof-follow-up'::text, 'complaint'::text, 'reported-problem'::text, 'rejected-command'::text, 'internal-task'::text, 'vehicle-defect'::text, 'other'::text])))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_number_key UNIQUE (company_id, number)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_origin_shape CHECK (((created_by IS NULL) = (source_event_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_parent_shape CHECK ((parent_ticket_id <> id))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_parent_ticket_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, parent_ticket_id) REFERENCES wms.ticket(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_pickup_shape CHECK (((pickup_id IS NULL) OR (route_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_priority_one_of CHECK ((priority = ANY (ARRAY['critical'::text, 'high'::text, 'medium'::text, 'low'::text, 'none'::text])))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_project_key UNIQUE (company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket ticket_property_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, property_id) REFERENCES wms.property(company_id, project_id, id)

constraint wms.ticket ticket_recollection_shape CHECK (((recollection_route_id IS NULL) OR (NOT (resolution IS DISTINCT FROM 'recollected'::text))))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_resolution_one_of CHECK ((resolution = ANY (ARRAY['recollected'::text, 'serviced'::text, 'answered'::text, 'no-action'::text, 'duplicate'::text])))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_resolution_shape CHECK (((status = 'completed'::text) = (resolution IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.ticket ticket_route_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_id) REFERENCES wms.route(company_id, project_id, id)

constraint wms.ticket ticket_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['open'::text, 'in-progress'::text, 'pending'::text, 'on-hold'::text, 'completed'::text, 'rejected'::text])))

relation wms.ticket_event kind=table owner=<owner> persistence=p rls=true force-rls=true replica-identity=d acl=[<owner>=DELETE,INSERT,MAINTAIN,REFERENCES,SELECT,TRIGGER,TRUNCATE,UPDATE/<owner> wms_api=INSERT,SELECT/<owner> wms_worker=SELECT/<owner>]
column wms.ticket_event 1 id uuid not-null default=wms.uuidv7()
column wms.ticket_event 2 company_id uuid not-null
column wms.ticket_event 3 project_id uuid not-null
column wms.ticket_event 4 recorded_at timestamp with time zone not-null default=now()
column wms.ticket_event 5 ticket_id uuid not-null
column wms.ticket_event 6 kind text not-null
column wms.ticket_event 7 status text not-null
column wms.ticket_event 8 assignee_user_account_id uuid
column wms.ticket_event 9 resolution text
column wms.ticket_event 10 body text
column wms.ticket_event 11 visibility text not-null default='internal'::text
column wms.ticket_event 12 object_key text
column wms.ticket_event 13 source_event_id uuid
column wms.ticket_event 14 recorded_by uuid
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_assignee_user_account_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, assignee_user_account_id) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_kind_one_of CHECK ((kind = ANY (ARRAY['created'::text, 'assigned'::text, 'status-changed'::text, 'comment'::text])))
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_kind_shape CHECK (
CASE kind
    WHEN 'created'::text THEN ((body IS NULL) AND (object_key IS NULL) AND (visibility = 'internal'::text) AND (resolution IS NULL))
    WHEN 'assigned'::text THEN ((object_key IS NULL) AND (visibility = 'internal'::text) AND (resolution IS NULL))
    WHEN 'status-changed'::text THEN ((object_key IS NULL) AND (visibility = 'internal'::text) AND ((status = 'completed'::text) = (resolution IS NOT NULL)))
    WHEN 'comment'::text THEN ((body IS NOT NULL) AND (resolution IS NULL))
    ELSE false
END)
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_recorded_by_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, recorded_by) REFERENCES wms.user_account(company_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_resolution_one_of CHECK ((resolution = ANY (ARRAY['recollected'::text, 'serviced'::text, 'answered'::text, 'no-action'::text, 'duplicate'::text])))
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_status_one_of CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['open'::text, 'in-progress'::text, 'pending'::text, 'on-hold'::text, 'completed'::text, 'rejected'::text])))
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_ticket_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, ticket_id) REFERENCES wms.ticket(company_id, project_id, id)
constraint wms.ticket_event ticket_event_visibility_one_of CHECK ((visibility = ANY (ARRAY['internal'::text, 'customer'::text, 'service-provider'::text])))
index wms.ticket_event ticket_event_assignee_user_account_id_idx CREATE INDEX ticket_event_assignee_user_account_id_idx ON wms.ticket_event USING btree (company_id, assignee_user_account_id)

column wms.unload 20 refusal_reason text

constraint wms.unload unload_company_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES wms.company(id)
constraint wms.unload unload_gross_kg_positive CHECK ((gross_kg > 0))
constraint wms.unload unload_location_valid CHECK ((extensions.st_isvalid(location) AND (NOT extensions.st_isempty(location)) AND (extensions.st_xmin((location)::extensions.box3d) >= ('-180'::integer)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_xmax((location)::extensions.box3d) <= (180)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_ymin((location)::extensions.box3d) >= ('-90'::integer)::double precision) AND (extensions.st_ymax((location)::extensions.box3d) <= (90)::double precision)))
constraint wms.unload unload_net_kg_positive CHECK ((net_kg > 0))

constraint wms.unload unload_project_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id) REFERENCES wms.project(company_id, id)

constraint wms.unload unload_route_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, project_id, route_id) REFERENCES wms.route(company_id, project_id, id)

constraint wms.unload unload_session_shape CHECK (((source = 'driver-app'::text) = (session_id IS NOT NULL)))
constraint wms.unload unload_source_one_of CHECK ((source = ANY (ARRAY['driver-app'::text, 'dispatch'::text, 'integration'::text])))
constraint wms.unload unload_tare_kg_positive CHECK ((tare_kg > 0))
constraint wms.unload unload_unloading_station_id_fk FOREIGN KEY (company_id, unloading_station_id) REFERENCES wms.unloading_station(company_id, id)

constraint wms.unload unload_weights_shape CHECK ((((gross_kg IS NULL) = (tare_kg IS NULL)) AND ((gross_kg IS NULL) OR (net_kg = (gross_kg - tare_kg)))))
